# Traditional Chinese artworks - study reference library

Study references only (composition and brushwork; everything is repainted new). Licence/reuse statements are as given by the source; Wikimedia Commons entries are photographic reproductions of works that are public domain by age (Commons tag shown). Every file was checked with `file` and ImageMagick `identify`; dimensions below are the real pixel size of the saved file. `manifest.json` carries the same data (plus `original_url` where Commons served a 3840-px-max thumbnail instead of the original).

## Counts

- `blocking/`: 42 files
- `ink_idiom/`: 67 files
- `monkeys/`: 47 files (japanese_prints: 4, woodcuts_li_zhuowu: 16, woodcuts_xiyouji: 15)
- `opera/`: 168 files (met_100_portraits_peking_opera: 100, shengpingshu_opera_faces: 46)
- `stone_and_script/`: 45 files
- `wushu_staff/`: 37 files
- `ledger_and_books/`: 16 files
- **Total: 422 images**

## Global notes

- **Resolution**: Wikimedia Commons stopped serving original files to our client part-way through (HTTP 429 on `upload.wikimedia.org` originals), so many Commons items are the largest allowed thumbnail (max 3840 px wide); for these the `note` field gives the true original size and `original_url` a link that redirects to the full file. Biggest known originals: Huizong Literary Gathering 12075x18343, Fan Kuan 11105x24176, Wang Ximeng complete scroll 39974x1600, Xia Gui Pure and Remote View 24557x1210. Met, Cleveland and Internet Archive items are full-size as served.
- **Sources actually used**: The Met Open Access (CC0), Cleveland Museum of Art Open Access (CC0), Internet Archive (National Library of China / CADAL scans of Ming books), Wikimedia Commons (NPM Taipei, Palace Museum Beijing, Nelson-Atkins, Princeton, Shanghai Museum etc. as mirrored there). The NPM Open Data portal, Palace Museum Beijing site, Tokyo National Museum ColBase, British Museum, Smithsonian/Freer, Library of Congress (blocked by a bot challenge) were not retrieved directly; the same works were taken from Commons where available.
- **Licences**: `Met Open Access CC0` and `Cleveland ... CC0` need no attribution. NPM Taipei Open Data images appear on Commons as CC0, Public domain or CC BY 4.0 (free reuse with attribution). Commons items tagged `CC BY` / `CC BY-SA` (a few photographs of paintings, cliff inscriptions, stone drums, a clan hall, a Wubeizhi plate) need attribution - and share-alike for BY-SA - if ever republished; fine as private study references. Internet Archive Ming/Qing books are public domain by age (the NLC volumes carry the PD Mark 1.0; the CADAL-sourced volumes state no rights).
- **Qi Baishi** (d. 1957): public domain in China since 2008 (life+50), but the US restored copyright status is uncertain (published in the 1950s, URAA); treat the two Commons images as study-only. **Zhang Daqian** (d. 1983) is not free; nothing downloaded - see list at the end.
- **Woodcut note**: the Journey to the West woodcuts here are the Wanli-era *Xinke chuxiang guanban dazi Xiyouji* (NLC, vols. 1-4) and the Li Zhuowu commentary edition (CADAL, vol. 1), not the 1592 Shidetang edition itself.


## A. blocking/ - the ten quoted compositions (+ companions)

#### `blocking/01_ma_lin_night_outing_candles_npm.jpg` - Night Outing with Candles (Strolling at Night Holding a Candle) / 秉燭夜遊圖
- Artist: Ma Lin 馬麟 | Date: c. 1250 | Holding institution: National Palace Museum, Taipei
- Pixel size: 960x1282 | Licence/reuse: Wikimedia Commons: CC0 (Creative Commons Zero, Public Domain Dedication)
- Source page: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File%3A%E7%B4%88%E6%89%87%E7%95%AB%E5%86%8A_%E5%86%8A_%E5%AE%8B%E9%A6%AC%E9%BA%9F%E7%A7%89%E7%87%AD%E5%A4%9C%E9%81%8A%E5%9C%96.jpg
- Direct image URL: https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/55/%E7%B4%88%E6%89%87%E7%95%AB%E5%86%8A_%E5%86%8A_%E5%AE%8B%E9%A6%AC%E9%BA%9F%E7%A7%89%E7%87%AD%E5%A4%9C%E9%81%8A%E5%9C%96.jpg/960px-%E7%B4%88%E6%89%87%E7%95%AB%E5%86%8A_%E5%86%8A_%E5%AE%8B%E9%A6%AC%E9%BA%9F%E7%A7%89%E7%87%AD%E5%A4%9C%E9%81%8A%E5%9C%96.jpg?utm_source=commons.wikimedia.org&utm_campaign=imageinfo&utm_content=thumbnail (original: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:FilePath/https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File%3A%E7%B4%88%E6%89%87%E7%95%AB%E5%86%8A_%E5%86%8A_%E5%AE%8B%E9%A6%AC%E9%BA%9F%E7%A7%89%E7%87%AD%E5%A4%9C%E9%81%8A%E5%9C%96.jpg)
- Note: Commons thumbnail (max 3840px wide); original is 1060x1416
- Look at: Fan-album leaf: tiny figure with lantern in huge dark empty garden/night; a few trees and rocks hold the vignette; corner-weighted asymmetry, light only from the candle

## Not found / substituted / low-res only

**Blocking works (A)**
- A1 Ma Lin, Night Outing with Candles 秉燭夜遊圖: found, but only 960x1282 (NPM CC0 scan as hosted on Commons; Commons original is 1060 px wide). Same painting is the "Waiting for Guests by Lamplight" file (`01b_...`, 960x911), NPM's English title - not a different work.
- A3 Muqi, Guanyin/Crane/Gibbons triptych (Daitoku-ji): **low-res only** - a 500x288 composite of all three panels and a 330x545 crop of the crane panel. No open high-res source exists (Daitoku-ji is not an open-access holder; ColBase does not hold it). Separate Guanyin and gibbon panels were not found.
- A9 Muqi, Six Persimmons (Daitoku-ji/Ryokoin): **low-res only**, 330x403 (Commons original 491x600).
- A4/A8: the "complete scroll" files are 3840 px thumbnails of 24557x1210 and 39974x1600 originals, i.e. only ~190 and ~154 px tall; use the section/detail files (3840 px wide, ~800 px tall) for brushwork. Originals can be fetched via `original_url` in `manifest.json`.
- A10: all five Met scrolls obtained (ten kings, two per scroll) with Met's extra detail images; Cleveland Ten Kings of Hell album leaves (Southern Song) added as companions.

**Elsewhere**
- Substituted/noted: Commons "Gibbons in a Landscape" turned out to be by the Japanese painter Sesson Shukei and was dropped; Ma Lin "Night Outing" alt file is the same painting, not a second work; shadow-puppet images are the Met's 19th-c. "Shadow player" set, none is a monkey king; Li Zhuowu woodcut chapter numbers are partly inferred (see notes).
- Not found as open images: Mao Song "Monkey" (Tokyo National Museum - ColBase not retrieved); anonymous Song gibbons beyond the Met/Cleveland ones; Muqi gibbon details; Yujian Mountain Market beyond a small Commons photo (1044x524); Gao Kegong (only a 785x470 teaching-page image); Ma Yuan Water Album: **only 4 of 12 leaves** (Commons); Qing painted album 《彩繪全本西遊記》; 1592 Shidetang original (Commons has only CADAL djvu volumes, not extracted); Journey-to-the-West nianhua (general Yangliuqing/Taohuawu prints supplied instead); paper-cuts of the monkey; Mei Lanfang facial-makeup scrolls; Yang Xiaolou/other pre-1930 monkey-actor photographs; 19th-c. export paintings of opera figures; monkey-king shadow puppets and puppet heads from Cleveland/Smithsonian/British Museum.
- Stone and script: no Mitsui Song Stone Drums scans (Met old rubbing and Commons rubbings used); no Yu He seal-and-clerical Thousand Character Classic, Zhao Mengfu six-script, or genuine Huizong Thousand Character Classic images (only one Commons file labelled as Huizong calligraphy, unverified); Fangshan stone sutras (1 photo), Jiangjunya (1 photo), 高山流水 at Huaguoshan (none); Qing collector seals only as the Huizong-collection seal impressions found on Commons.
- Wushu: no Ming original woodcuts of Cheng Zongyou's staff forms or Mao Yuanyi staff plates were obtainable at usable resolution. Supplied: Qi Jiguang *Jixiao xinshu* Siku-copy plates (spear, pole drills, boxing), the 1921 Shanghai edition's staff-posture plates (modern redrawn figures), and five low-res Wubeizhi weapon plates from Commons. LoC Wubeizhi scans exist on Commons (22 volume-spreads of ~6300 px, ids `Wu bei zhi LOC 2004633695-N`) but were not sorted for staff pages.
- Ledgers: no Ming yellow-register or fish-scale-register images found; supplied Ming thread-bound book spreads (genealogy, Journey to the West front matter), the 868 Diamond Sutra (BL, via Commons), Dizang/Ten Kings (BM 2147x3005, Guimet 1192x1476). Dunhuang Ten Kings Sutra illustrated scroll (BM/BnF Pelliot) not found.
- **Zhang Daqian splashed-colour**: not downloaded (copyright until 2053); consult museum/auction catalogues. Qi Baishi: see global note.
- Library of Congress (its search API returned a non-JSON, likely bot-challenge response), Academia Sinica, Smithsonian/Freer, British Museum, Princeton and Nelson-Atkins sites were not queried directly (others skipped for time).
